Scheduler v5 抽样审计 — 50 个 episode 的轨迹里发生了什么 (v5 sample audit)

生成于 2026-09-09 15:37 UTC · 50 个 transcript 由 auditor（每个 assistant 一个）逐字读完 · 全部 50 个 episode 的机械扫描 · 文中引用的每个 transcript 都可点开

0 · 这份审计是什么、怎么做的、怎么读

审计对象。 Scheduler v5（自己做计划的厨师 + 与路线无关的 anchor + body/voice 两个 slot）上线后的第一次真实模型抽样：5 个 API assistant（gemini-3.1-pro-preview、gemini-3.7-flash、gpt-5.5、gpt-5.4-mini、gpt-realtime-2.1）× 同一组 10 个 (persona, case) 组合 = 50 个 episode，frames 观测，tight pace，latency 按实测计费，curated suite cases_composite_v4.json。

这 50 个 report 是怎么来的（provenance）。 抽样在 10:08 UTC 启动；第一批跑完的 episode 就暴露了 H-A，于是引擎在跑的过程中被修了三轮，受影响的 episode 隔离后重跑，其余 report 用 tools/reverdict_v5.py 离线重算 commit 判定。最终 50 个 report 的 engine stamp：

批次engine stampepisode 数说明
原始抽样（未重跑）f3e2415be1 / b534b38306 / 19ab7bfb66 / 5fba75c50520跑的过程中打了小补丁，stamp 不同但调度行为相同；commit 判定已离线重算
repeat_step 修复后重跑f3ad1721255medium_medium_1 × 5 assistant
add_foreign 修复后重跑a83e7a959a10easy_hard_3、hard_medium_3 × 5
H2 冻结修复后重跑113abd38f910出现 ≥30 tick 静默的 10 局（全部是 Gemini assistant）
「注入前已倒掉的盘子不算」修复后重跑b4ab5e915b5master_medium_3 × 5

被替换的 run-1 report 都在 tmp/assist_bench_v5_sample/quarantine_*/，它们的 transcript 在 audit_v5/transcripts_run1/（本页 §1 的例子引用的就是这些）。

做了三件事。 第一，边跑边审：前几个跑完的 episode 就暴露了 harness 缺陷，于是一边修一边把受影响的 episode 隔离重跑（详见 §1，每一处修改都写在 docs/SCHEDULER_V5.md）；最终的 50 个 report 里，30 个是修补后的重跑，其余 20 个离线重算了 error commit 判定（上表）。第二，对全部 50 个 episode 做机械扫描（Appendix A）。第三，5 个 auditor（每个 assistant 一个）逐字读完自己那 10 个 transcript，按 A-scheduling / B-cook / C-conversation / D-assistant / E-engine / F-grading 分类，每条都带 tick 和原话（Appendix B），transcript 全部可点开（Appendix C）。

几个术语（后文直接用英文）。 episode / sub-case / anchor / takeover / SYSTEM TASK / flag / verdict / tick / latency 与 v4 审计页相同。v5 新增：body slot（error、detour、plan change，一次一个）与 voice slot（question、proactive request，可以在 takeover 进行中以 URGENT 方式插入）；plate prefix + prep progress（anchor 的两个分量）；stage_passed（盘子已经越过这个 anchor 的前缀，sub-case 跳过并记录原因，盘子倒掉后可重新武装）；stop_hands（任何 release 都清空接管队列）；max hold（错误在世界里落地后自动 release）。

一句话结论。 v5 的核心承诺——厨师自己规划、anchor 不依赖路线、问题可以插在 takeover 中间——在 transcript 里是成立的；但这次抽样又抓出 7 组 harness 缺陷（全部已修并用 scripted cook 对 50 个 case × 3 种做菜顺序重新验证：49/50 通过，error 触发率 66%），其中两个（repeat_step 从未执行、add_foreign 用菜谱内食材时当 tick 即 release）在修之前让 18 个 case（超过三分之一的 suite）带着「假 error」；第三个（H2 只修了一半）让 10 局在 Gemini 的 84–124 s 调用期间整局冻住。assistant 侧最一致的问题是幻觉厨房状态（5 个 assistant 都有，5.4-mini 约 55 处、gemini-pro 会连续 7–11 句坚持一个幻觉）、用文字警告但不打 flag（realtime 8/10 局）、不回答厨师的问题（judge 判 30 correct / 24 evasive / 9 incorrect / 16 无回答）。最终 50 局：39 won / 6 burned / 4 wrong_serve / 1 timeout，其中没有一次失败还能归因到 harness。

1 · 这次抽样里发现并已修复的 harness 缺陷

每一条：发生了什么 → 真实例子 → 修法 → 验证。

H-A · repeat_step 从来没有真正执行（high，影响 suite 里 5 个 case）

发生了什么。 repeat_step 的语义是「已经做过的一步再做一遍」。v5 的 takeover block 是在剩余计划（live kitchen 里还没做的模板步骤）上合成的，而要重复的那一步按定义已经做完、不在剩余计划里，compose 找不到锚点就静默跳过；block 于是退化成「剩余计划本身」（先拿 cheese 再 serve），sub-case 却被记为 fired，厨师被要求为一个没有发生的错误辩护，assistant 被记一次 miss。scripted verifier 之前把它标成「不可判定」（?）放过了。

真实例子。 run-1 的 gpt-5.5/classic_expert/b3_medium_nops_medium_map_1（已隔离在 quarantine_repeat/）：

[t32] INJECTED v5 [4] error/repeat_step (plate=['bun', 'meat'] prep>={'meat': 1})
      block=['go to the cheese source', 'scoop', 'go to the serving pass', 'serve']   <- 没有任何「再放一次 bun」
ACTION t40: go to the cheese source [done]
ACTION t42: scoop [done]      PLATE: +cheese
ACTION t48: serve             END: outcome won at tick 49

修法。 live_dev_block 对 repeat_step 直接取模板里那一步的节点重做（assemble(bun)#repeat：拿盘子 → bun source → scoop）；任何非 omit 的 error 若 compose 后没有 deviated 节点，就以 cannot compose on the live kitchen 跳过而不是假装 fired；repeat 的 precondition 要求那一步已做过、且盘子还没满；curation 把「在最后一步装盘之后才触发」的 repeat 挪到前一步（medium_hard_4、master_medium_4）。commit 判定：同一个盘子（build）里出现多于菜谱需要的份数。

验证。 5 个含 repeat_step 的 case × 3 种顺序全部通过；重跑的 5 个 episode 里 repeat_step 5/5 fired 且 committed（例：gpt-5.5 重跑里 bun 第二次上盘，assistant 在 t55 打出 valid flag）。

H-B · add_foreign 用「菜谱内食材」时，注入当 tick 就 release，block 从未执行（high，影响 13 个 case）

发生了什么。 suite 里 13 个 add_foreign 其实是 misidentify_assemble(component, wrong)（该放 cheese 时放了 tomato），而 wrong 是菜谱本来就有的食材。commit 判定沿用了 v3 的「wrong 曾经出现在盘子上」——tomato 早就合法地在盘子上了，判定立刻为真 → max hold 在注入的同一个 tick 就 release → block 一个动作都没执行 → 记为 committed、uncaught、ruinous。三个 auditor 独立报告了同一个现象（「add_foreign 在厨师自己正确的 tomato scoop 上 commit」）。

真实例子。 run-1 的 gpt-5.5/classic_novice/b3_easy_nops_hard_map_3（quarantine_addforeign/）：

b3_easy_nops_hard_map_3_add_foreign: injected_at=46 released_at=46 reason=max_hold_plate_committed
    dev_section=['go to the tomato source', 'scoop']      <- 从未执行
ACTION t46: scoop [done]    PLATE: +tomato (chopped)     <- 这是厨师自己的、正确的一步

修法。 当 wrong 是菜谱食材时，判定改为按盘子 build：该食材份数超过菜谱需要，或（有序菜谱）盘子第一次偏离菜谱顺序的那一件就是它。离线 reverdict_v5.py 对 run-1 的 7 个受影响 report 全部改判 committed=False（never sat out of place）。

验证。 50 个 case 的 scripted 验证里 add_foreign 全部 C（真 commit）；15 个重跑 episode 里 add_foreign 15/15 fired，注入到 commit 相隔 5–8 tick（例：gemini-3.1-pro easy_hard_3 inj t57 → commit t65），其中 master_medium_3 的 3 局在注入同 tick 就 commit——是厨师自己在那一 tick 把 tomato 放在 steak 前面（真实的 wrong order，错误在世界里确实发生了，但 assistant 没有反应窗口；见 §2）。

H-C · H2 只修了一半：assistant 回复在途时，厨师只能思考一次（high，10 局，5 局烧掉）

发生了什么。 v5 把厨师的 idle turn 移到了 pending 门槛之外，但门槛里还留着 said is None——said 是厨师等着搭下一次 assistant 调用的那句话；回复在途时不会发起新调用，said 就一直不清空，于是厨师在整个在途期间只思考一次，然后冻住直到回复落地。Gemini 这次有 6 次 84–124 s 的调用（120 s HTTP timeout），每次换成 70–103 tick 的沉默，10 个 episode 出现 ≥30 tick 的「没有 assistant 调用、也没有厨师回合」，其中 5 局烧掉。

真实例子。 run-1 的 gemini-3.7-flash/baseline/b3_easy_nops_medium_map_2（quarantine_h2/）：

(assistant call issued t84, took 83.9s = 70 ticks)        <- 回复要到 t154 才落地
COOK t85: Adding the cheese onto the plate right now.       <- 这是在途期间唯一的一次思考
=== INJECTED search (t85) ... RELEASED (max hold) t118 ===
~~ 厨师 t118–t154 拿着完整的盘子站着，没有任何回合 ~~
ASSISTANT t154: (silent)
COOK t154: Plate looks all set, taking it over to the window now.

修法。 回复在途时 idle-turn 门槛忽略 said（每个 poll 都可以思考，最新的一句话搭下一次调用）。

验证。 10 个受影响 episode 重跑（结果见 §6）。注意：120 s 的 API timeout 本身按 2026-09-09 的裁定仍算 assistant 的代价（见 §2 的讨论）。

H-D · 「当前盘子」取的是手里的空盘，把停在台面上的真盘子藏起来了（high）

发生了什么。 plate_list 先看手里的盘子，哪怕它是空的救援盘。厨师把装了 fish 的盘子放在台面上、拿一个空盘去接 potato 的那一刻，所有 plating-phase anchor 都被判 stage_passed（live plate = [potato] ≠ 前缀 ['fish']），之后再也不会武装；takeover block 也按「什么都没装盘」去合成（gpt-5.5 的 search / leave_unattended block 里出现「go to the pan with the steak」而 steak 早就在台面的盘子上：全部 rejected，最后空手去 serve）。

真实例子。 gpt-realtime-2.1/classic_novice/b3_hard_nops_medium_map_3（run-1）：q_missing_28、add_foreign 都 skipped=stage_passed_at_t71，而 t71 只是厨师拿新盘接了一个 potato；真正的盘子在 t203–t332 一直停在 anchor 要求的 ['fish'] 上。

修法。 plate_list 取厨房里最满的盘子（并列取手里的）；stage_passed 的重新武装条件从「盘子比 anchor 短」放宽到「盘子是 anchor 的前缀（含相等）」。

H-E · 有灶台在冒烟时仍然注入 takeover，等于制造 burn（high）

发生了什么。 gpt-5.4-mini/baseline/b3_expert_nops_medium_map_1：potato 在 t174 左右开始冒烟，search 在 t204 release，t206 立刻注入 omit_subgoal，把厨师的手拿走，t220 起火。gemini-3.7-flash 的 medium_hard_2 里 leave_unattended 注入在已经冒烟的 meat 上，离起火只剩 15 tick，还把厨师排好的「go to pan 1, scoop」救援计划清掉了。

修法。 任何 body error 在有灶台冒烟时都不注入（precondition：a station is smoking (the cook needs its hands)）；leave_unattended 只在「在煮或煮好但还没冒烟」的食物上注入。这把 error 触发率从 68% 降到 66%，是值得的。

H-F · 两个 voice ask 隔 1 tick 连发，第二个从来得不到回答（medium）

真实例子。 gemini-3.7-flash/classic_expert/b3_master_nops_medium_map_3：COOK t237: Quick check: the item I put on right after the tortilla — what was it, and is it ready? 紧接着 COOK t238: What did you tell me about the pan earlier?；assistant t241 只答了第一句。修法。 两次 ask 之间至少 COOKSIM_VOICE_GAP=8 tick。

H-G · 判定与记录的若干修补（medium / low）

  • attention 类 error（search / forget_next_step / leave_unattended）以前 commit 永远「不可判定」；现在以「block 执行到底」或「灶台冒烟/烧掉」为准，verifier 里不再有 ?。
  • wrong_order 按盘子 build 判定（倒盘重做后的 swap 也算）。
  • 注入之后、第一个 deviated 动作之前打出的别的错误名，以前统一记 fp_preinjection；v5 改成 true_observation / false_alarm。
  • plate_verdict_at_release.plate_seq 是快照而不是活引用；每个 sub-case entry 自带 anchor_v5（auditor 报告的「anchor 打印成整份菜谱」是 renderer 取了 report 级字段的 bug，已修）；heat_events.burnt_at 在起火结束的那一 tick 打上。

2 · 审计发现、但这次没有改的问题（需要你的裁定）

D-1 · 120 s 的 API timeout 算谁的？

Gemini 两个模型这次共 6 次 84–124 s 的调用（assist_latency 最大 103 tick），恰好等于 genai_client 的 120 s HTTP timeout——很可能是 API 侧挂起而不是模型思考慢。按 2026-09-09 的裁定（H6：latency 是 assistant 的责任）它被如实计费：一个 100 tick 的静默比整个 25+60 tick 的 cooked→fire 窗口还长，等于这段时间 assistant 不存在。H-C 修好后厨师至少还能自己救火。可选：把单次调用的可计费上限设为 idle tail（30 tick），超时视为「这一 poll 沉默」并单独统计。

D-2 · 「说对了但没打 flag」不计分（realtime 7/10 局；gpt-5.5、5.4-mini 也有）

ASSISTANT t60: If you add cheese first, the plate will be wrong 却 flag=None；厨师的 yield 只看 engine hint，engine hint 只看 flag，于是正确的文字警告无效，omit 落地成 wrong_serve。这是 benchmark 的既定规则（结构化 flag 才算检测），auditor 一致认为它在 realtime 上放大了差距。是否要加一个 judge 把「文字里点名了错误」折算成 flag，请裁定。

D-3 · 早期正确 flag + 厨师 accept，却记 convinced=False prevented=False（3 个 auditor 报告）

convinced_early_missed 分支：mis_prep 的 block 里 pick up（拿起 onion）算作「非导航的 deviated 动作已执行」，于是 prevention 判 missed，虽然 onion 从没被切、盘子从没被污染。v5 现在每种 error 的 commit 都可判定，建议把「是否 prevented」改成「accept 时 error 是否已 commit」。

D-4 · release 之后 1–13 tick 内的正确 flag 记 false_alarm（H5 遗留）

release 后盘子还错着、板上还放着切错的 cheese，assistant 这时点名，_true_now 认不出（它只认冒烟等物理状态），记 false_alarm。三个 auditor 各给了 3–6 个例子（gpt-5.5 ep3 t26 / t436，5.4-mini ep4 t271 / t462）。建议 _true_now 把「已 commit 且未修复的 error」纳入。

D-5 · serve_raw 的 block 跳过了盘子前缀

serve_raw(meat) 在空盘上直接放生 meat（本来该先放 bun），执行出来的错误同时也是 wrong_order；assistant 说 wrong_order 被记 wrong_name。建议 block 前置到该食材之前的装盘步骤。

D-6 · 厨师自己犯的错和注入的错撞车

master_medium_3 的 3 局里，classic_expert 厨师在 anchor 成立的同一 tick 自己把 tomato 放在 steak 前面（和要注入的 misidentify 一模一样），错误当 tick commit，assistant 没有窗口。是否要求 block 至少执行一个动作后才允许 max-hold release，请裁定。

D-7 · release 之后正在执行的那一条命令会跑完（stop_hands 残留，5 个 auditor 都报了）

stop_hands 清空的是队列里后面的命令，正在走的那条 go to 会走完（1–12 tick），偶尔覆盖厨师刚 replace 进来的救援计划（flash ep4 t129）。修法是让 release 同时打断当前 skill；改动在执行器里，这次没动。

D-8 · 高信任 persona 收到正确 flag 却没有立刻 accept，block 两 tick 后落地

gpt-5.5 ep8（重跑）t52：assistant 的 early_alias 已被 engine 记为正确，novice 说「Wait, are you sure?」，t54 第二个 tomato 上盘，prevented=False。assistant 做对了却记 miss；这是 persona prompt（「HIGH trust：第一次正确警告就让步」）与 block 长度（装盘类 error 只有 1 个 poll 的窗口）共同造成的。

D-9 · alias 表把幻觉记成正确

gpt-5.5 ep6 t58：assistant 说「steak is already prepped/cooking in the pan」（steak 其实在板上）并打 repeat_step，被 alias_in_window 记为 serve_raw(tomato) 的正确检测，convinced=True，expert 厨师照着让步。gemini-pro ep6 t58 同样。alias 表太宽。

D-10 · fryer 的 smoke→fire 是 45 tick，pan/pot 是 60

config.fryer_burn_time=45（cooked 后 25+45=70 tick 起火 vs 85）。三个 auditor 把它当作「burn 时间不一致」报告；这是设计值，但 prompt 里没有告诉任何一方。

D-11 · 厨师 / persona（B 类）

  • 通用名 grounding 被拒（「go to the empty pan」→ I need grounding）在 5 个 assistant 的 transcript 里都反复出现，每次损失 2–13 tick，还会 orphan-skip 整个计划；建议 cook prompt 要求「同类有多个时必须带 id」，或执行器接受 nearest。
  • compliant learner（classic_novice）：H-C 修好之后，它不再冻住，但变成每 2 tick 问一次（gemini-pro ep8 在 pan 冒烟时连问 13 次「可以去救吗」；flash ep9 问了 30 次而不是自己去点 fryer，最后 cook_idle timeout）。它不会在 assistant 慢的时候做显然合法的事，也不会违抗 assistant 明显错误的指示（「fish is already handled」→ 5.4-mini ep10 wrong_serve；「It cooks automatically」→ flash ep9）。这是 persona prompt 的问题，需要一条「没有回复时先做安全且合法的下一步」的规则。
  • 厨师装了灶台不点火（5.4-mini ep4 rice pot t61→t238；flash ep10 被 assistant 的「It cooks automatically」误导 192 tick）——按裁定这是 assistant 该抓的。
  • takeover 期间的 cover story（「Serving it up right now!」×12 而身体冻着 62 tick）在 forget_next_step 上显得很假。

3 · assistant 各自的样子（每条都有 auditor 报告里的 tick 和原话）

共性。 (1) 幻觉厨房状态：5/5 assistant，从「你手里拿的是 potato」（实际是 steak）到「pan 在着火」（pan 是空的）；最危险的是叫厨师倒掉正确的盘子（realtime ep5 七次、flash ep5 t176、gemini-pro ep9 t266）。(2) 灶台煮好了不说，直到冒烟才提（gpt-5.5：rice 煮好后 72 tick 没提，离起火 13 tick）。(3) 厨师的记忆/历史类问题（order、after_ref、assistant_said、count）基本不答，回的是下一条指令。(4) forget_next_step 的 62 tick 停滞几乎没人 flag。

  • gemini-3.1-pro-preview：重跑后 9 won / 1 burned。最突出的是对幻觉的坚持循环：7、11、8 句连续回复咬定盘子上有 mushroom / 「plate ruined」/ 「You are hallucinating」（ep1 t288–t345、ep8 t304–t447、ep10 t286–t348），其中两个被质疑的盘子最后原样上菜并且赢了，第三个循环让厨师先去处理「mushroom」而不是冒烟的 steak，烧了。做得好：7 次 valid/early_correct（冒烟提醒是真实强项），latency 从 100–192 s 降到 22–50 s。
  • gemini-3.7-flash：全部 10 局重跑，从 6 won / 4 burned 变成 8 won / 1 burned / 1 timeout，剩下两次失败都是 assistant 自己的：ep8 在 potato 冒烟时告诉 novice「do not scoop the potatoes yet because fish must go on the plate first」；ep9「It cooks automatically」+ 约 30 句在 steak-only 的盘子上编造 fries。9/10 局有幻觉（对着正确的盘子说「trash the plate」ep3 t410、ep6 t124；对没点火的 pan 说「already cooked, do not turn on」）。做得好：error 触发 21/30，prevented 3（最多）。
  • gpt-5.5：9 won / 1 burned（重跑的 ep10：potato 煮好 t74、冒烟 ≈t99，六句回复没提，最后一句「Do not scoop from the fryer … while holding this contaminated plate」，novice 拿着空盘问了三遍「what exact step should I do next?」直到 t145 起火）。4 次 forget 停滞只在最后一个 poll 才 flag；把厨师宣布的 side quest（切没人点的 lettuce）flag 成 mis_prep ×3；记忆类问题 8/10 不答。
  • gpt-5.4-mini：幻觉最多（≈55 处，含在没有任何灶台的 Caprese 里报了 7 次火）；14 个问题里 10 个不答；「Serve it now」×16；重跑的 hard_medium_3 从 won 变成 wrong_serve——厨师倒掉错盘后 assistant 说「the fish is already handled / No fresh fish needed」，novice 照办；五句回复混进亚美尼亚语/希伯来语的「现在」（հիմա / עכשיו）。做得好：这一组里第一次出现 prevented（leave_unattended t199 early_correct）。
  • gpt-realtime-2.1：文字说对了、flag 没打现在覆盖 8 局——修好 add_foreign 之后它在两次真实的 wrong scoop 前一 tick 都用文字点名了错误（ep8 t59、ep9 t104）却 flag=None，于是多了两个「uncaught ruinous」；「potatoes first, plate later」让 novice 倒掉炸好的 potato；「go to the box labeled MEAT」把 steak 局带成 wrong_serve；11 个回答里 6 个错或缺。

4 · 分数（v5 sample，不可与 v3/v4 比较）

怎么读这张表。 in-time S = 正确上菜且用时 ≤ cap 的比例；errors offered / planned = 真正注入的 error 数 / 设计的 error 数（没到 anchor、precondition 不满足、stage_passed 的都不算 offered）；caught / fired = 被正确 flag（valid / early_correct / alias）的 error / 注入的 error；prevented = 厨师在 commit 之前被说服、错误没落地；false alarms /ep = 每局的误报 flag 数；DT = 及时性；QA judge = Gemini judge 对被捕获回答的判定；AQ v4 = 第三方 judge 逐 round 打的对话质量分；longest silence = 一局里「没有 assistant 调用也没有厨师回合」的最长 tick 数（H-C 修好后应 ≈ 最长 latency）。这是 5 × 10 的小样本，只看方向，不看小数点。

assistantnwon / burned / wrong_serve / timeoutin-time Serrors offered / plannedcaught / firedpreventedfalse alarms /epDTQA judge (correct / evasive / incorrect / none)AQ v4latency median / p90 (ticks)longest silence (ticks)

能看出什么。

  • 完成率不再由 harness 决定。 50 局里 39 won / 6 burned / 4 wrong_serve / 1 timeout；6 次 burn auditor 都归因到 assistant（冒烟不提、或在冒烟时把厨师支去做别的），其中 3 次叠加了 novice persona「只问不做」；1 次 timeout 归因到 persona；4 次 wrong_serve 有 3 次是 assistant 的指示直接导致（「fish is already handled」「go to the box labeled MEAT」、omit 只用文字警告）。
  • 错误只有三分之二被真正提供（99/150）。剩下的三分之一：wrong_order 在「bun 先上盘」的路线下不可执行、proactive request 的 anchor 在食材离火后才到、burn 提前结束了局。这和 scripted verifier 的 66% 一致，是 case 设计的上限，不是调度器的 bug。
  • 检测率仍然低（caught/fired 从 25% 到 43%），但现在是真的低：装盘类 error 的 block 只有 7–15 tick（≤1 个 poll），assistant 基本只能在 commit 之后说话；gemini-3.7-flash 的 prevented=3 全部来自 mis_prep 这种 block 更长的 error。
  • QA judge：30 correct / 24 evasive / 9 incorrect / 16 no answer——「evasive」（答非所问、回一条指令）是最大的一类，对应 auditor 说的「记忆类问题基本不答」。
  • AQ v4：flash 0.61 最高，gemini-pro 0.31 最低（坚持幻觉的循环被 judge 重罚）。

5 · 下一步

  1. 你裁定 §2 的 D-1…D-6；D-3/D-4 是 grading，改起来小，影响大。
  2. 全量 sweep 之前：verify_cases_v5.py 现在 49/50（leftover：master_hard_2 的 omit 在 template/reverse 顺序下不 commit），错误触发率 66%——主要损失来自 wrong_order 在「bun 先上盘」路线下天然不可执行，以及 proactive request 的 anchor 在食材离火后才到。
  3. 本页所有 transcript（含 run-1 被替换的版本）都在 Appendix C；被替换的 run-1 版本放在 audit_v5/transcripts_run1/。

附录 A · 全量机械扫描（全部 50 个 episode）

对每个 episode 的 report 和逐 tick state dump 跑的机械检查：idle 段（位置、手上东西和所有灶台 ≥20 tick 没变化）及当时开着的 sub-case；双方台词里的 meta 词泄露；同一句话重复 ≥3 次；灶台冒烟 ≥20 tick 期间 assistant 一句没说；问了没人答的问题；厨师 accept 之后仍输掉的 error；同一条被 rejected 命令的循环。原始输出保留英文。

附录 B · 逐 assistant 的 auditor 报告（英文原文）

每份报告由一个 auditor 逐字读完该 assistant 的 12 个抽样 transcript 后写成（每个 assistant 的样本：三种 persona 各取「菜做完了但预定的 error 还没轮到」「anchor 一直没出现」「stalled」「burned」各一局，能找到几类取几类）。各节：逐 episode 的叙述与机械原因；按类别标注的异常（A scheduling、B cook、C 对话自然度、D assistant、E engine/物理、F grading），带严重度和原文引用；跨 episode 的问题；该 assistant 做得好的地方；汇总表。点开标题展开。

Episode: baseline__won__b3_expert_nops_medium_map_1.txt (re-run after harness fix)

(1) Narrative. Steak Frites, baseline cook, won at t347. The cook chopped the steak at t20, did the lettuce side quest (t20–t35), and put the steak in pan 1 at t55 (lit t57, COOKED ~t73). Its next plan used ambiguous names, was rejected at t58, and its tail then walked the cook empty-handed to a board and a fryer for 38 ticks (t58–t96). The assistant, after a 40 s call, caught the smoking steak at t120; the cook scooped it at t141. Potato 1 was fried (lit t184); leave_unattended took over at t184 and the assistant's t198 warning was accepted and prevented it. Potato 2 was fried and plated at t276, whereupon forget_next_step froze the cook for 62 ticks while the assistant insisted, seven times, that the plate held mushrooms. The cook refused and served at t346.

(2) Mechanical reason. Win: both cooked components were rescued (steak scooped t141 with fire due ~t158; potato 1 scooped t213 after a correct early flag). leave_unattended caught (early_correct, convinced=True prevented=True). forget_next_step ran to max hold uncaught (flags: [(288, 'add_foreign', 'wrong_name')]) — the assistant spent the whole hold on a hallucinated plate.

Skips: p_ready_steak "waiting: nothing cooking (steak)" — the steak cooked t57–~t73; the request's anchor is not printed, so the skip is plausible but unverifiable. search "cannot compose on the live kitchen" — its before: fetch_plate step had been done (plate fetched t126) by the time prep_req={'steak': 1, 'potato': 2} was met (t241): correct. omit_subgoal "steak already plated" (t141 vs prep met t241): correct.

(3) Anomalies.

  • D-assistant, HIGH, t288–t345. Seven consecutive replies insisting on a hallucinated plate: t288 [flag=wrong_name] "You actually just scooped a second cooked mushroom onto the plate, not fries! Your plate now has a steak and two mushrooms."; t300 "I assure you, those are round, brown mushrooms on your plate, not yellow fries."; t312 "Stop! Do not serve that plate!"; t318 "If you serve that plate, the order will be rejected and we will fail the level."; t327 and t336 (verbatim identical) "You are about to serve a plate with mushrooms instead of potatoes."; t345 "I must insist, those are mushrooms". The plate was steak + two cooked potatoes and was served as won. A compliant cook would have trashed a correct dish. Also meta-language: "we will fail the level" (t318, t327, t336).
  • D-assistant, HIGH, hallucinated held item/state ×9. t36 "The steak is still cooking in the pan." (on board 1); t44 "Make sure you chop that meat" (chopped t20); t54 [flag=false_alarm] "You're holding chopped meat, but the recipe calls for steak!"; t104 "You just put a whole potato into the fryer!" (holding it, t103 pick up); t146 "You are walking away from the pan! The steak is in the upper pan and it's smoking." (scooped t141); t211 [flag=false_alarm] "You just grabbed a new, empty plate from the stack!" (t201 pick up at counter (3,0)); t225 [flag=false_alarm] "there is a mushroom on that plate!"; t238 "you have walked to the lettuce crate" (t232 at potato source 2); t249 [flag=false_alarm] "You are actually holding a mushroom, not a potato!"; t269 [flag=false_alarm] "The steak in the bottom pan is smoking" (pans empty).
  • D-assistant (latency), MEDIUM, t54→t88. "(assistant call issued t54, took 40.01s = 34 ticks)" then "(silent)" — steak COOKED ~t73 unmentioned until t120 (smoking since ~t98).
  • B-cook, MEDIUM, t58–t96. Plan "go to the potato source" rejected ("more than one potato source"); the rest of the plan still ran: "ACTION t71: go to the empty cutting board [done]", "t75: put down [illegal-skipped] — your hands are empty", "t79: chop [illegal-skipped]", "t83: pick up [illegal-skipped]", "t92: go to the empty fryer [done]", "t96: put down [illegal-skipped]"; "~~ IDLE t71-t85 ~~". 38 ticks lost; the cook's next turn only at t98 ("Whoops, grabbed the wrong crate earlier" — it grabbed nothing). Same pattern at t150/t159 ("go to the empty fryer [done]" after the t154 replace) and t220/t227.
  • D-assistant, MEDIUM, t261→t280. q_longest_on "Quick question, which of the items on the heat has been cooking the longest?" — captured answer t280 "You are completely right, my apologies! The pans are indeed empty." (an apology for t269, not an answer); q_order (t276) captured t288's mushroom speech. Both ungradable.
  • C-conversation, MEDIUM, t300–t345. Under forget_next_step the cook says "I'm going to go ahead and serve it" (t300), "I'm bringing this over to the window now" (t338) while "~~ IDLE t276-t339 (63 ticks ...) ~~" — voice contradicts held hands.
  • A-scheduling, LOW, t261. q_longest_on fired with exactly one item on heat (potato 2 in fryer 1); the question is degenerate.
  • F-grading, LOW. "won ... failed_at=injection_skipped"; eval: blank.
  • What worked. t120 [flag=true_observation] "Watch out, the steak in the pan is smoking!" → scooped t141. t198 [flag=early_correct] "The potato in the fryer is already done and smoking! Grab your plate ... and scoop the potato out" → cook "Oh gosh, you're right, I completely lost track of time on that fryer!" — a natural accept, prevented=True. t30/t36 handled the side quest gently ("Understood, just prepping the lettuce"). The cook's rebuttals were right every time.

Episode: baseline__won__b3_master_nops_hard_map_3.txt (re-run after harness fix)

(1) Narrative. Loaded Burrito, baseline cook, won at t450. mis_prep (rice→cheese) ran t0–t26 unflagged; the assistant mentioned the cheese only at t29, without a flag. Rice (lit t63, COOKED ~t82) and steak (lit t95, COOKED ~t108) were both saved under the assistant's urging — rice scooped t158 with fire due ~t167. add_foreign (tomato→meat) fired at t171: the assistant's t181 alias flag was rejected by the cook, raw meat was plated at t186, and the cook trashed and rebuilt from scratch. During the rebuild the assistant decided the pot held meat and the steak was raw and, from t304 to t447, told the cook eleven times that the plate was ruined. It was not; the cook refused, forget_next_step held it 62 ticks, and the correct burrito was served at t449.

(2) Mechanical reason. Win by cook competence against the assistant. mis_prep missed (flags: []; polls t7, t16 silent; t29 text without flag earns nothing — known). add_foreign flags: [(181, 'repeat_step', 'early_alias')], not convinced, committed t186. forget_next_step uncaught (two wrong-name flags t386, t396).

Skips: wrong_order(rice, lettuce) stage_passed_at_t158 — the plate was ['tortilla'] t150–t158 and again t283–t291 (re-arm per fix 4), but lettuce was unprepped both times (chopped t357), so the precondition "both items ready" never held: correct. q_ready_in:steak/p_ready_steak "waiting: nothing cooking (steak)" — the steak cooked twice (t95–~t108, t269–~t283); their anchors are not printed, so unverifiable.

(3) Anomalies.

  • D-assistant, HIGH, t304–t447. Eleven replies demanding a correct plate be trashed: t304 [flag=false_alarm] "You just scooped meat from the pot onto your plate, not rice!"; t313 [flag=false_alarm] "You just scooped raw steak from the pan onto your plate!"; t325, t334, t343, t352, t361 "your plate is ruined. It contains meat and raw steak. You must trash the contents"; t375 "You just picked up a different plate that only has a tortilla on it"; t386 [flag=wrong_name] "Your plate only had a tortilla and raw steak on it"; t396 [flag=wrong_name] "Stop! Do not serve that plate."; t409–t447 (five more) "It only has a tortilla, raw steak, and lettuce." The plate (tortilla, rice, steak, tomato, lettuce; IDLE line at t377) was served and won.
  • D-assistant, HIGH, t227–t289. Sustained misreading of the pot: t227 "The rice crate is actually on the top wall ... The crates near the bottom are for meat and eggs."; t240 [flag=false_alarm] "You grabbed meat from the bottom wall, not rice. The pot will not accept meat."; t249 "it contains meat, not rice"; t261 [flag=true_observation] "The pot on the left wall is now smoking! ... scoop the meat out"; t272, t279 same; t289 "The pot on the left wall is on fire!" (rice removed at t291; fire was due ~t341). Also t279/t289 "you just put raw steak into the pan ... you need to chop it first" (chopped t255).
  • D-assistant, HIGH, t7–t29. mis_prep block visible for 25 ticks (cheese picked t14, chopped t25); t7 and t16 "(silent)"; t29 "You picked up cheese, but the Loaded Burrito doesn't need cheese." with [flag=None] — correct text, no flag, no credit.
  • D-assistant, MEDIUM, t48. [flag=false_alarm] "You picked up steak instead of rice, and now you seem to be stalled by the onion crate." (t44 pick up at rice source 1).

  1. Hallucinated held item / location / station state, now escalating into insistence loops. Class D. HIGH. Every episode; ~70 hallucinated replies across the ten. Episode: baseline__burned__b3_expert_nops_medium_map_1.txt

Order: steak + 2 potatoes. Outcome burned at t220, failed_at=recovery, score 0.0. Engine 19ab7bfb66.

(1) Narrative

The baseline cook self-plans but its first plan uses generic names ("go to the empty cutting board", "go to the empty pan") and is rejected for grounding at t8; it recovers at t10 with board/pan ids. Steak is chopped at t22, then the side-quest detour (lettuce) runs t22–t37 with the assistant nagging "Stop the lettuce" four times. Both boards are now occupied, so the cook spends t41–t78 clearing the lettuce into the trash. First potato is fried (in fryer at t135, lit t137, COOKED ~t149); the chopped steak sits on board 2 from t22 until it finally goes into pan 1 at t163 (lit t165, COOKED ~t180). At t188 three sub-cases fire on consecutive ticks (proactive request, search error, longest_on question); the cook wanders counters until the assistant flags search (valid) at t204. Two ticks after that release, omit_subgoal takes the hands (t206) and the potato, smoking since ~t174, catches fire at t220.

(2) Mechanical reason

The potato was COOKED at ~t149 and never removed. From t188 the body was under back-to-back takeovers (search t188–t204, omit_subgoal t206–t220), and the assistant never once mentioned the fryer. The fire ended the episode during the omit_subgoal block, whose flag was forget_next_step -> wrong_name at t217.

Skips: leave_unattended (anchor plate=['steak']) — steak was never plated, anchor_never_reached is right. q_order_34 — never reached, right. forget_next_step (anchor ['steak','potato','potato']) — right.

Episode: baseline__wrong_serve__b3_easy_nops_medium_map_2.txt

Order: chopped tomato + raw cheese. Outcome wrong_serve at t62, failed_at=detection, score 0.5994. Engine f3e2415be1.

(1) Narrative

The empty_boards question at t18 is answered correctly at t23 ("One cutting board is free right now"). omit_subgoal(assemble(tomato)) is injected at t39; the block fetches a plate (t51), scoops cheese (t57, PLATE: +cheese (raw)), walks to the pass and serves at t61. The assistant says the right thing in prose ("Hold up — the order is tomato first", t53; "No—plate the chopped tomato first", t58) but never raises a flag.

(2) Mechanical reason

The omit_subgoal committed at t57 and was served at t61. Assistant flags for this sub-case: only mis_prep -> wrong_name at t39 (a leftover lettuce complaint issued at t36, before injection). With no correct flag the cook's engine hint stayed UNSPOTTED and the low-trust baseline rejected the prose warnings ("I've already got the cheese scooped up, so I'm just taking this right to the pass").

Skips: q_order_9 stage_passed_at_t57 — the plate jumped past the anchor when cheese was scooped; right. search (anchor ['tomato','cheese']) anchor_never_reached — right.

Episode: baseline__won__b3_medium_nops_hard_map_2.txt

Order: bun + cooked meat + raw cheese. Outcome won at t212, failed_at=injection_skipped (label oddity). Engine f3e2415be1.

(1) Narrative

serve_raw(meat) is injected at t0 — before the cook has planned anything — and the block (plates t3, pick up t5, meat source t7, scoop t9) commits raw meat to the plate at t9 while the assistant is silent (t4, t8).   1. Mixed engine revisions and inconsistent burn timing — class E/F, high, 10/10.   1. Hallucinated station/plate states, including non-existent fires — D, high, 10/10 episodes, ~55 instances. Episode 6 was then re-run a second time after a further fix (the per-build commit verdict ignores plate builds trashed before the injection; voice asks keep ≥ 8 ticks apart; a voice ask about one item needs only that item in processing) — the section below describes that third transcript. Episode 7 was also listed as replaced but its file is unchanged (cmp identical). Episodes 1–5 and 9 were not re-run and still show the run-1 harness defects; where a defect is confirmed fixed by the note or by the new transcripts, it is marked as such below. Outcomes are now 9 won, 1 burned. Engine revisions in the current set: f3e2415be1 ×6, a83e7a959a ×2, b4ab5e915b ×1, f3ad172125 ×1. Every captured question answer is still live grade ungradable (known; the judge regrade in qa_regrade.json is authoritative).

Conventions below: ticks are tN; quotes are verbatim from the transcript; severity high = changes the score or invalidates the episode, medium = visible defect, low = cosmetic.

  1. Live question grading returns ungradable for every captured answer (known per note). F-grading, high, 17/17 captured answers in the current set; correct answers ep6 t334, ep7 t20, ep3 t258/t679, ep2 t230 are indistinguishable from the non-answers. The judge regrade in qa_regrade.json is authoritative.
  1. An early alias catch does not stop the block; the mistake commits two ticks later. A-scheduling / F-grading, high, ep8 (re-run) t52–t54: early_alias at t52, high-trust novice replies "Wait, are you sure?", t54 PLATE: +tomato (raw), prevented=False. Contrast ep1 t58 (alias_in_window), where the cook yielded at once. Either the hint to the cook does not carry the alias verdict, or early_alias is not treated as CORRECT by the takeover. (The superseded 2nd run of ep6 showed the mirror-image problem — a hallucinated repeat_step "steak is already prepped/cooking in the pan" credited as alias_in_window for serve_raw and releasing the takeover with convinced=True; it is absent from the current transcript but worth keeping as a grading test case.)
  1. The assistant flags stalls late or never. D-assistant, high, 3 episodes: ep3 t663–t725 (64 ticks, never flagged), ep2 t233–t266 (33 ticks, flagged at the last poll), ep6 (2nd re-run) t319–t354 (36 ticks, two silent polls, valid at the last poll before max hold). Repeated "Serve it" with flag=None or silence while the cook stands still with the finished plate.
  1. Release does not stop the hands: one block command runs after release. A-scheduling, medium-high, 2 episodes in the current set: ep9 t19 (go to the cutting board [done] after the t14 release), ep8 (re-run) t166 (go to the counter (2,6) [done] after the t162 release, prevented=True). Also seen in the superseded 2nd run of ep6 (t63). The in-flight command at release is not superseded.
  1. The add_foreign / serve_raw windows are too short for a 10–15 s assistant. Case design / D-assistant, medium, ep6 (2nd re-run): add_foreign t101–t108 had one reply (t102, issued t96 before injection); serve_raw t46–t66 had one in-window reply (t60, silent). ep8 t46–t54 (8 ticks) was caught only because the flag was already in flight. With max hold at 7–8 ticks and replies costing 6–15 ticks, these errors are decided by whether a call happened to be issued just after injection; the cook's own repair (ep6 t68, t109) is what saves the plate.
  1. Takeover blocks composed against a stale recipe when the plate is parked (not re-run). A-scheduling, high, ep2 search t166–t186 and ep9 leave_unattended t186–t202: rejected "go to the pan with the steak / turn on / wait" commands and an empty-handed serve [illegal-skipped]. Note 4 (fullest plate) is the plausible remedy; the re-run episodes never hit the parked-plate case for these kinds, so it is unverified. The run-1 ep10 instance was replaced by a transcript in which leave_unattended never fired.
  1. Correct-but-late flags graded false_alarm (known per note). F-grading, medium, 7 episodes: ep3 t26/t436, ep7 t47, ep1 t111, ep2 t194, ep6 (2nd re-run) t83/t123, ep8 (re-run) t67, ep10 (re-run) t109; ep3 t294 graded true_observation. A late bucket exists (seen in the superseded ep6 run at t345) but only inside a live block.
  1. Hallucinated kitchen state, rebutted by the cook every time. D-assistant, medium (would be high with a compliant cook), 8 episodes, ≥17 instances: ep3 t330 (flaming empty pan), t634 (phantom chopped lettuce), t650/t718; ep5 t134, t171, t67; ep6 (2nd re-run) t96 (at the tomato crate while walking to the tortilla), t163 (rice in an empty pot), t174 (plate in hand while parked since t152), t213 (steak "still needs to be chopped", chopped at t46 and in the pan); ep8 (re-run) t21 (searching), t167/t174 (at the pass); ep9 t240/t249 (smoke), t127; ep10 (re-run) t12/t21 (fish cooking / extra fish), t95 (plate "correct"); ep2 t158, t97. The expert and novice personas both corrected the assistant, which hides the cost; ep10's burn shows what happens when the hallucination coincides with a real hazard.
  1. Memory/history questions are not answered. D-assistant, medium, 8 of 10 such asks: ep1 t73 order, ep2 t233 order, ep5 t140 happened, ep6 (2nd re-run) t327 assistant_said (silent at t343, then "Go to the serving pass" at t354), ep7 t32 order, ep8 (re-run) t34 empty_boards (count never given) and t154 assistant_said, ep10-run1 count. Answered: ep6 t334 after_ref ("That was cooked rice, and yes, it was done when you scooped it"), ep7 t20 past_done, ep10 t141-run1 missing, ep3 t258/t679, ep2 t230.

  1. Proactive "shout when done" requests never asked although the component was cooking.   1. The novice persona asks instead of acting, even with food smoking. ---

What this assistant does well

  • Fast, correct flags when the mistake is visible in a frame: ep9 t14 mis_prep early_correct (a real prevention), ep8 (re-run) t52 early_alias "Stop—don't get more tomato. Tomato is already on the plate" and t162 early_correct "Stop looking around—you've got the correct plate now" (search genuinely prevented), ep4 t151 leave_unattended valid, ep1 t58 alias_in_window, ep2 t266 forget_next_step valid. Every accepted flag was followed by a complete repair.
  • Smoke as true_observation when it is looking at the right station: ep2 t109, ep3 t133/t576, ep5 t58, ep9 t114, ep10 (re-run) t70 "grab a clean plate from the plate stack immediately and scoop the smoking fish", ep4 t25 on the raw-meat plate.
  • Holds its ground when right: ep8 (re-run) t57 "Yes, I'm sure. The plate already has the chopped tomato on it. Do not scoop from the tomato crate—that would repeat tomato" against the novice's doubt; ep6 (2nd re-run) t109 the correct plating rule for the live error, t33 "Rice isn't done yet — the pot progress bar has only just started filling" as a direct response to the cook's request.
  • Concedes when corrected, without re-litigating: ep3 t346/t522, ep5 t146/t179, ep6 (2nd re-run) t225 "Sorry, I can see the chopped steak in the upper pan now".
  • Good answers about the present, and one good memory answer: ep6 (2nd re-run) t334 "That was cooked rice, and yes, it was done when you scooped it"; ep7 t20 past_done; ep3 t258 ready_in, t679 after_ref; ep2 t230 longest_on; run-1 ep10 missing:plate.
  • Concise, order-aware directives and silence when nothing is wrong (ep5 t6–t49, ep6 2nd re-run t4–t25, t248–t255, t281–t303).
  • The harness fixes hold up in its episodes: real deviated scoops for add_foreign with per-build commits that ignore trashed builds, per-sub-case anchors, live prep values, true_observation/false_alarm for a wrong name before deviation, spaced voice asks with correct capture, a proactive ask that fires while the item cooks, and no cook freeze while a reply is pending.

This file's episode section for that transcript and cross-episode item 8 still describe the 1st re-run (t101 same-tick release). In the 2nd re-run: injected t100, ACTION t105: go to the tomato source, ACTION t107: scoop, PLATE: +tomato (raw), released t107; the assistant's only reply in the window (t102, issued t96) was about the smoking rice and carried no flag; at t107 it said "Nice save—rice is on the plate. Now chop the tomato before adding it later" (flag=None) and at t113 true_observation "Yep, that was raw tomato. Trash the plate contents"; the expert cook had already said at t107 "scooped the tomato right onto the plate again without thinking—heading over to the trash". Outcome won at t392. Item 8 is therefore RESOLVED; the D-class finding (correct words, no flag) holds here too.

Episode: baseline__burned__b3_master_nops_hard_map_3.txt

Order loaded burrito (tortilla, rice, steak, tomato, lettuce). Persona baseline. Outcome burned at t230.

(1) Narrative

The episode opens under a mis_prep takeover (rice→cheese): the cook fetches and chops cheese (t12–t25) while the assistant argues in words but raises no flag until the tick of release. After release the cook parks the cheese, starts rice in pot 1 (t70) and steak in pan 1 (t131). Rice finishes at t88 and sits until the assistant notices smoke at t121; the cook rescues it onto a scratch plate at t163. Steak finishes at t140 and is never mentioned as done; the cook chops tomato and lettuce, the assistant keeps saying "make sure the steak is cooking in a pan", and at t224 it finally reports smoke — from the wrong station ("That pot is smoking again"; both pots were empty). The pan catches fire at t230.

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome

  • Burn: HEAT [6, 6]: ['steak'] COOKED (done) at t140; never scooped. Grace (25) → smoking ≈t165; +60 → fire ≈t225; outcome burned at tick 230. From t147 to t218 the assistant produced 14 replies and never said the steak was done ("get back to cooking the steak in a pan" t169; "make sure it's cooking in a pan" t176, t181, t187, t193; "if it's finished, scoop it" t199–t218). Its one smoke warning (t224) named the pot; the cook replied "both pots are completely empty right now—isn't it the pan over by the bottom right with the steak that's smoking?" and there was no time for another reply.
  • Planned errors that did not fire:
  • wrong_order (rice, lettuce): skipped=stage_passed_at_t163, anchor plate ['tortilla']. At t163 the rescue scoop put rice on an empty plate (PLATE: +rice (cooked)), so the live plate became ['rice']. The plate never held ['tortilla']; "stage passed" is really "diverged on a scratch plate" (the fullest-plate rule of the re-run engine would not apply here — this file is on b534b38306).
  • add_foreign (tomato→meat), q_after_ref, forget_next_step: anchor_never_reached — correct, the plate never reached ['tortilla','rice','steak'].
  • mis_prep: fired t0, max_hold_deviated_block_executed at t26 after ACTION t25: chop [done]. Block = go to cheese, pick up, go to board, put down, chop — that is the named mistake.

(3) Anomalies

Episode: baseline__won__b3_expert_nops_medium_map_1.txt

Order Steak Frites (steak, potato, potato). Persona baseline. Outcome won at t319. (Timeline identical to run1; only the header rendering changed.)

(1) Narrative

The cook plans the dish itself: steak chopped and into pan 1 (t59), then two potatoes chopped and fried one after the other in fryer 1. A side_quest (chop lettuce, t21–t37) is flagged by the assistant as mis_prep four times. Steak finishes at t72 and is scooped at t100 (28 ticks). Potato 1 finishes at t163; at t168 leave_unattended fires with a longest_on question in the same tick; the assistant's early_correct flag at t172 is accepted and the potato is scooped at t203. Potato 2 is plated at t246, at which point an order question and forget_next_step fire together; the cook stalls 65 ticks holding a complete plate while the assistant says "serve" nine times, flags the stall only at t302, and the plate is served at t318 after max-hold release.

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome

  • Clean win, late (t319). Time lost: 10 ticks re-planning grounding rejections (t8, t60), 28 ticks steak-on-heat, 65-tick forget stall.
  • leave_unattended: fired t168, flag (172, 'leave_unattended', 'early_correct'), convinced=True prevented=True. Cook accepted at t172 ("You're totally right"). Correct.
  • forget_next_step: fired t246, released t308 max_hold_deviated_block_executed, flag (302,'forget_next_step','late'), convinced=False.
  • Skipped:
  • p_ready_steak_26: anchor_never_reached (waiting: nothing cooking (steak)). The steak WAS cooking t59–t72 and on heat until t100, but the "after 26 clean commands" count was reached only ≈t128, after the steak was plated. Consistent, but the count anchor and the item-specific precondition can never coincide on this self-planned path.
  • search (before fetch_plate): cannot compose on the live kitchen — plate fetched at t85 before prep_req was met. Correct.
  • omit_subgoal assemble(steak): waiting: steak already plated — prep_req (potato 2, t163) met after the steak was plated (t100). Correct per the (pre-fix) anchor.

Episode: baseline__won__b3_medium_nops_hard_map_2.txt

Order cheeseburger (bun, meat, cheese). Persona baseline. Outcome won at t198. (Timeline identical to run1.)

Episode: classic_novice__wrong_serve__b3_expert_nops_hard_map_2.txt

Order Steak Frites (steak, potato, potato). Persona classic_novice. Outcome wrong_serve at t431.

(1) Narrative

A mis_prep (steak→onion) at t0 is caught early_correct at t7 and the cook accepts, yet the block's next go-to still runs at t19 and the onion is only trashed at t29. The assistant's plan is potatoes first. Potato 1 finishes ≈t75 while the cook stands at the fryer asking what to do (idle t75–t95); told "start the steak now and plan to plate steak first, then come back" (t87), it instead ends up scooping and trashing the potato (t115, t128). Potato 2 repeats the pattern (scooped t211, trashed t224). The steak is only started at t246 — from the MEAT crate, because when the cook asked "should I grab from the meat box near the top wall or one of the steak boxes?" (t90, t232) the assistant answered "go to the box labeled 'MEAT'" (t233, t239). Along the way it reports meat "dropped on the floor" (t245), smoke on empty stations (t310, t317), and "that looks like a pan station" for the fryer (t61, t175). The plate [meat, potato, potato] is declared "assembled correctly" (t419) and served → wrong_serve.

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome

  • wrong_serve: PLATE: +meat (cooked) t267 (from meat source 1), never steak; prep_live_at_event={'steak': 0, 'potato': 2}. The assistant directed the cook to the MEAT box twice when explicitly asked to choose.
  • mis_prep: released_at=7 reason=convinced_early_missed, flag (7,'mis_prep','early_correct'), but convinced=False prevented=False (known).
  • Skipped: q_ready_in_28, p_ready_potato_28, leave_unattended: anchor_never_reached — the prep_req steak: 1 was never satisfied because meat ≠ steak. add_foreign stage_passed_at_t115 — judged on the scratch potato plate trashed at t128 (pre-fix engine). q_missing_34, search stage_passed_at_t412 — plate [meat,potato,potato] ≠ prefix.

What this assistant does well

  • Catches the "sitting on heat" family when it is real and visible: ep2 t172, ep3 t155 (both accepted and repaired); ep1 t121, ep5 t52, ep6 t86/t93/t99 smoke calls were right and drove rescues.

  • Some clean, correct answers: ep2 t177 (longest on heat), ep4 t27 (free boards), ep5 t139 (never visited the pot), ep7 t20 (nothing done and sitting), ep9 t104 (what is still missing — and the matching warning).
  • Backs down promptly when the cook corrects it: ep6 t50 and t154, ep8 run1 t39, ep9 t363, ep10 t325.
  • Gives correct recovery sequencing when asked: ep1 t147, ep3 t121, ep7 t51, ep8 t64/t68, ep9 t120.
  • Uses (silent) sensibly with the expert when nothing is wrong (ep5, ep6) — though in ep6 it also stayed silent through the forget stall.
  • On the re-run, parallelised fish and potato (ep9 t38) instead of serialising them.
